ls20 是怎么学出来的 · 程序化世界模型
程序化世界模型
各局方法册
← ls20 的世界模型

它是怎么学出来的

agent 边玩边把看到的规律写成可检验的代码：每条都对接下来的每一步自动判对错。它一共写过 22 条，其中 3 条被后来的画面推翻、换成了新的写法。通关之后，这些说法被重新整理成 21 条规则：18 条进了模型，3 条在录像上逐步检验。整理时合并了不少：上下左右各一条的「走一格」合成了一条；「扣能量」的两条合成了一条按关查单价的规则。

每条规则的来历

  • 目标格是普通地板，玩家走进那一格
    由这些早期说法合并而来：move_up、move_down、move_left、move_right
  • 目标格贴着弹簧线，玩家会被继续推着滑
    由这些早期说法合并而来：move_up、move_down、move_left、move_right
  • 撞墙、撞上钥匙不对的门，原地不动
    由这些早期说法合并而来：passable
  • 过关，当且仅当走进钥匙对上的最后一道门
    由这些早期说法合并而来：levelup_iff_match
  • 走进对上的门、但不是最后一道：这道门消失
    由这些早期说法合并而来：frame_consumed
  • 能量用光再走，掉一条命
    由这些早期说法合并而来：death_reset、refill
  • 踩十字开关，钥匙顺时针转一个直角
    由这些早期说法合并而来：toggle_rotates_hud
  • 踩非十字开关，钥匙换成下一种形状
    由这些早期说法合并而来：toggle_maps_hud
  • 踩染色物件，钥匙换成下一种颜色
    由这些早期说法合并而来：paint_recolors_hud
  • 什么都没踩，钥匙不变
    由这些早期说法合并而来：toggle_changes_hud、toggle_rotates_hud、paint_recolors_hud
  • 每按一次方向键，扣掉这一关的单价
    由这些早期说法合并而来：energy_cost、energy_tick
  • 走进补给，能量回满
    由这些早期说法合并而来：refill
  • 巡逻的开关：玩家走一步，它沿线走一格
    由这些早期说法合并而来：mover_shifts
  • 绕圈的染色物件：玩家走一步，它沿圈走一格
    由这些早期说法合并而来：paint_orbits
  • 有没有迷雾，由关卡决定
    由这些早期说法合并而来：fog_disc
  • 静止的开关和染色物件不会自己挪，补给只会变少
    由这些早期说法合并而来：refill_vanishes
  • 不开门的一步，门不变
    由这些早期说法合并而来：frame_consumed
  • 能量没用光，命数不变
    由这些早期说法合并而来：death_reset
  • 同一关里，地板和弹簧线不变不进 Lean 模型
    由这些早期说法合并而来：passable、move_up、move_down、move_left、move_right
  • 掉命之后，这一关从开局重来不进 Lean 模型
    由这些早期说法合并而来：death_reset
  • 按重开键，回到第 1 关开局不进 Lean 模型
    由这些早期说法合并而来：—

被推翻的说法

踩开关不是镜像，是转直角

toggle_mirrors_hud
  1. 原来以为

    第 1 关第一次踩十字开关，钥匙的图样看上去像是左右翻了一面。只有一次观测，agent 照最省事的解释写下：踩开关，钥匙左右镜像。

  2. 画面打脸

    第一遍游玩的第 41 步再踩一次开关，新图样不是旧图样的镜像，而是把它顺时针转了一个直角。

  3. 后来改成

    改成：踩十字开关，钥匙顺时针转一个直角。后来 Lean 把这件事钉成了定理：转四次回到原样，转两次一般回不来——镜像两次就会回来，所以它不是镜像。

门要的不只是形状，还有颜色；也不是哪道门都能过关

levelup_iff_match
  1. 原来以为

    前两关的钥匙和门一直是同一种颜色，agent 写下：钥匙形状和门里一样，走进门就过关。

  2. 画面打脸

    有一步钥匙的形状已经和门一样，颜色却不同，玩家走过去被挡在门外。后来一关里同时出现两道门，走进对上的那一道，门消失了，关卡却没换。

  3. 后来改成

    改成：钥匙的形状和颜色都要对上才进得去；只有走进盘上最后一道门才过关，别的门走进去只会消失。

附录

附录保留仓库里的原始叫法，关号从 0 开始（L0 = 正文第 1 关）。

另外 2 个改写的故事

非十字开关「把钥匙设成门的图样」

toggle_sets_hudtoggle_maps_hud
  1. 原来以为

    第一次踩非十字开关，钥匙恰好变成了门里的图样。agent 写下：踩它，钥匙变成门要的样子。

  2. 画面打脸

    第一遍游玩的第 262 步，另一关里踩同样形状的开关，输入和输出与上一关完全相同，门要的图样却不同——结果和门无关。

  3. 后来改成

    先退一步，只登记见过的「这个开关、这把钥匙 → 那把钥匙」对照表；通关后整理规则时，这张表被改写成：几种形状排成固定的一圈，踩一下换成下一种，朝向不变。

每步扣几格能量，公式被推翻两次

energy_cost
  1. 原来以为

    第 1 关每步扣的少，第 2 关每步扣的多，agent 写成「关卡号越大扣得越多」的直线。

  2. 画面打脸

    第 3 关每步扣的和第 2 关一样多；改成「第 1 关少、之后都一样」，到第 4 关又扣得和第 1 关一样少。

  3. 后来改成

    放弃找公式，改成每一关登记一个单价，没见过的关不下结论。

全部 22 条早期说法的每一版与源码

from _lib import energy, lives, me, FULL_ENERGY

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if energy(prev) != 0:
        return "NA"
    if lives(prev) == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "FALSE"
    if lives(nxt) != lives(prev) - 1:
        return "FALSE"
    if energy(nxt) != FULL_ENERGY:
        return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if a != b else "FALSE"

from _lib import energy, lives, me, target_cells, FULL_ENERGY, REFILL

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if energy(prev) != 0:
        return "NA"
    if lives(prev) == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None or any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc):
        return "NA"
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "FALSE"
    if lives(nxt) != lives(prev) - 1:
        return "FALSE"
    if energy(nxt) != FULL_ENERGY:
        return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if a != b else "FALSE"

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, will_move, levels_up, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def _cost(ctx):
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None:
        return None
    return int(ctx["level_prev"]) + 1


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    k = _cost(ctx)
    if k is None or ctx.get("level_next") != ctx.get("level_prev"):
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None or a == b:
        return "NA"
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None or tg:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == max(0, e0 - k) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return None
    k = _cost(ctx)
    if k is None or levels_up(prev, action):
        return None
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if mv is None or tg is None or not mv or tg:
        return None
    row = prev[HUD_ROW]
    cols = sorted(c for c, v in enumerate(row) if v == HUD_COLOR)
    if not cols:
        return None
    out = {}
    for c in cols[:k]:
        out[(HUD_ROW, c)] = FLOOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, c)] = FLOOR
    return out
v2 · 2026-09-15T16:22:32 · L4
ACTION1–4 让 me 移动时 energy 减 k：移进不含开关色的目标区 k = level_prev + 1；移进含开关色的目标区 k = 2 × level_prev（第 0 关踩开关免费、第 1 关扣 2）；能量条从左端缩，减到 0 为止。
v1 只管普通步、线性 level+1。v2：把踩开关的步也收进来——两关读数 0 与 2，写成 2×level；与 level+1 在第 2 关分得开（3 对 4），让第 2 关考。

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, will_move, levels_up, alive, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def cost(prev, action, ctx):
    """这一步该扣几格；算不出返回 None。"""
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None:
        return None
    lv = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return None
    return 2 * lv if tg else lv + 1


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") != ctx.get("level_prev"):
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None or a == b:
        return "NA"
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if k is None:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == max(0, e0 - k) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if levels_up(prev, action):
        return None
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if mv is None or not mv or k is None:
        return None
    row = prev[HUD_ROW]
    cols = sorted(c for c, v in enumerate(row) if v == HUD_COLOR)
    if not cols:
        return None
    out = {}
    for c in cols[:k]:
        out[(HUD_ROW, c)] = FLOOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, c)] = FLOOR
    if k == 0:                                  # 免费的一步：认领最左那格不变，让「没扣」也能被判错
        out[(HUD_ROW, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
    return out

v1 只管普通步、线性 level+1。v2：把踩开关的步也收进来——两关读数 0 与 2，写成 2×level；与 level+1 在第 2 关分得开（3 对 4），让第 2 关考。

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, target_cells, will_move, levels_up, alive, REFILL, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def cost(prev, action, ctx):
    """这一步该扣几格；算不出返回 None；目标区含 REFILL 也返回 None（那是 refill 管的）。"""
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None:
        return None
    lv = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None or any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc):
        return None
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return None
    return 2 * lv if tg else lv + 1


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") != ctx.get("level_prev"):
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None or a == b:
        return "NA"
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if k is None:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == max(0, e0 - k) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if levels_up(prev, action):
        return None
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, target_cells, will_move, levels_up, alive, REFILL, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def cost(prev, action, ctx):
    """这一步该扣几格；算不出返回 None；目标区含 REFILL 也返回 None（那是 refill 管的）。"""
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None:
        return None
    lv = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None or any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc):
        return None
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return None
    if lv == 0:
        return 0 if tg else 1
    return 2


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") != ctx.get("level_prev"):
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None or a == b:
        return "NA"
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if k is None:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == max(0, e0 - k) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if levels_up(prev, action):
        return None
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if mv is None or not mv or k is None:
        return None
    row = prev[HUD_ROW]
    cols = sorted(c for c, v in enumerate(row) if v == HUD_COLOR)
    if not cols:
        return None
    out = {}
    for c in cols[:k]:
        out[(HUD_ROW, c)] = FLOOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, c)] = FLOOR
    if k == 0:                                  # 免费的一步：认领最左那格不变，让「没扣」也能被判错
        out[(HUD_ROW, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
    return out

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, target_cells, will_move, levels_up, alive, REFILL, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def cost(prev, action, ctx):
    """这一步该扣几格；算不出返回 None；目标区含 REFILL 也返回 None（那是 refill 管的）。"""
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None:
        return None
    lv = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None or any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc):
        return None
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return None
    if lv not in COST_BY_LEVEL:
        return None
    k = COST_BY_LEVEL[lv]
    return 0 if (tg and lv == 0) else k


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") != ctx.get("level_prev"):
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"

    if a is None or b is None or a == b:
        return "NA"
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if k is None:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == max(0, e0 - k) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if levels_up(prev, action):
        return None

        return None

    for c in cols[:k]:
        out[(HUD_ROW, c)] = FLOOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, c)] = FLOOR

        out[(HUD_ROW, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, target_cells, will_move, levels_up, alive, REFILL, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def cost(prev, action, ctx):
    """这一步该扣几格；算不出返回 None；目标区含 REFILL 也返回 None（那是 refill 管的）。"""
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None:
        return None
    lv = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None or any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc):
        return None
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return None
    if lv not in COST_BY_LEVEL:
        return None
    k = COST_BY_LEVEL[lv]
    return 0 if (tg and lv == 0) else k


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") != ctx.get("level_prev"):
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b and int(ctx["level_prev"]) not in BLOCKED_COSTS:
        return "NA"
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if k is None:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == max(0, e0 - k) else "FALSE"

    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if levels_up(prev, action):
        return None
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if mv is None or not mv or k is None:
        return None
    row = prev[HUD_ROW]
    cols = sorted(c for c, v in enumerate(row) if v == HUD_COLOR)
    if not cols:
        return None
    out = {}
    for c in cols[:k]:
        out[(HUD_ROW, c)] = FLOOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, c)] = FLOOR
    if k == 0:                                  # 免费的一步：认领最左那格不变，让「没扣」也能被判错
        out[(HUD_ROW, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
    return out

COST_BY_LEVEL = {0: 1, 1: 2, 2: 2, 3: 1, 4: 2, 5: 1, 6: 2}
BLOCKED_COSTS = {4, 6}   # 这些关里撞墙不动也照扣（第 6 关第 927/928 步；第 4 关第 1260–1268 步 7 次扣 2）；第 0/2 关撞墙不扣（各 1 次观测）

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, target_cells, will_move, levels_up, alive, REFILL, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def cost(prev, action, ctx):
    """这一步该扣几格；算不出返回 None；目标区含 REFILL 也返回 None（那是 refill 管的）。"""
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None:
        return None
    lv = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None or any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc):
        return None
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return None
    if lv not in COST_BY_LEVEL:
        return None
    k = COST_BY_LEVEL[lv]
    return 0 if (tg and lv == 0) else k


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") != ctx.get("level_prev"):
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b and int(ctx["level_prev"]) not in BLOCKED_COSTS:
        return "NA"
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if k is None:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == max(0, e0 - k) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if levels_up(prev, action):
        return None
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    k = cost(prev, action, ctx)
    if mv is None or not mv or k is None:
        return None
    row = prev[HUD_ROW]
    cols = sorted(c for c, v in enumerate(row) if v == HUD_COLOR)
    if not cols:
        return None
    out = {}
    for c in cols[:k]:
        out[(HUD_ROW, c)] = FLOOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, c)] = FLOOR
    if k == 0:                                  # 免费的一步：认领最左那格不变，让「没扣」也能被判错
        out[(HUD_ROW, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, cols[0])] = HUD_COLOR
    return out

from _lib import energy, me, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    expect = e0 - 1 if a != b else e0
    if energy(nxt) != expect:
        return "FALSE"
    if nxt[HUD_ROW + 1] != nxt[HUD_ROW]:
        return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    """只在能判断「会移动」时生成：靠 passable 概念的判定；判不出就不开口。"""
    if action not in ACTS:
        return None
    from _lib import will_move
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    if mv is None:
        return None
    row = prev[HUD_ROW]
    cols = [c for c, v in enumerate(row) if v == HUD_COLOR]
    if not cols:
        return None
    c = min(cols)  # 实测从左端缩
    if mv:
        return {(HUD_ROW, c): FLOOR, (HUD_ROW + 1, c): FLOOR}
    return {(HUD_ROW, c): HUD_COLOR, (HUD_ROW + 1, c): HUD_COLOR}

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, will_move, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR, FLOOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def _expect_drop(prev, action):
    """True = 这一步该扣 1；False = 不扣；None = 算不出。"""
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if mv is None or tg is None:
        return None
    return bool(mv and not tg)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return "NA"
    moved = a != b
    expect = e0 - 1 if (moved and not tg) else e0
    if energy(nxt) != expect:
        return "FALSE"
    if nxt[HUD_ROW + 1] != nxt[HUD_ROW]:
        return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"

    if action not in ACTS:
        return None
    drop = _expect_drop(prev, action)
    if drop is None:
        return None
    row = prev[HUD_ROW]
    cols = [c for c, v in enumerate(row) if v == HUD_COLOR]
    if not cols:
        return None
    c = min(cols)  # 实测从左端缩
    v = FLOOR if drop else HUD_COLOR
    return {(HUD_ROW, c): v, (HUD_ROW + 1, c): v}

from _lib import energy, me, steps_on_toggle, HUD_ROW

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    e0 = energy(prev)
    if e0 == 0:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    if tg is None:
        return "NA"
    e1 = energy(nxt)
    if nxt[HUD_ROW + 1] != nxt[HUD_ROW]:
        return "FALSE"
    if (a != b) and not tg:
        return "TRUE" if e1 < e0 else "FALSE"

有一关只看得见玩家周围一圈
第 6 关（FOG_LEVELS）：主区（行 < 55）里离 me 中心欧氏距离 > FOG_R + 1.5 的格在 nxt 里全是色 FOG（迷雾）；其余关不受本条约束。
v1 · 2026-09-16T04:25:02 · L7
第 6 关（FOG_LEVELS）：主区（行 < 55）里离 me 中心欧氏距离 > FOG_R 的格在 nxt 里全是色 FOG（迷雾），≤ FOG_R - 1 的格都不是迷雾色 5（框边除外：本关没有框可见时按此）；其余关不受本条约束。
第 926–929 步：第 6 关帧外圈全是色 5，圆盘随 me 移动；实测可见格最远 20.6、迷雾最近 13.0（欧氏），格子是 5×5 对齐的所以边界粗。半径取 19 是从这四步读的，别的读数就让它挨打。

import math
from _lib import me

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}
FOG_LEVELS = {6}
FOG = 5
FOG_R = 19.0


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") not in FOG_LEVELS or ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    b = me(nxt)
    if b is None:
        return "NA"
    cy, cx = b[0] + 2, b[1] + 2
    for r in range(55):
        for c in range(64):
            d = math.hypot(r - cy, c - cx)
            if d > FOG_R + 1.5 and nxt[r][c] != FOG:
                return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"

from _lib import me, goal_frames, consumes_frame, hud_pattern, hud_ink, alive, will_move, FLOOR, CELL

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def _region(f):
    return [(f[0] + dr, f[1] + dc) for dr in range(7) for dc in range(7)]


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    fs = goal_frames(prev)
    if not fs:
        return "NA"
    f = consumes_frame(prev, action)
    if f is None:
        return "TRUE" if goal_frames(nxt) == fs else "FALSE"
    if hud_pattern(prev) != hud_pattern(nxt) or hud_ink(prev) != hud_ink(nxt):
        return "FALSE"
    if [x for x in fs if x != f] != goal_frames(nxt):
        return "FALSE"
    mine = {(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}
    return "TRUE" if all(nxt[r][c] == FLOOR for (r, c) in _region(f) if (r, c) not in mine) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    """消框那一步认领框 7×7 里 me 没盖住的格：全变 FLOOR。"""
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev) or me(prev) is None:
        return None
    f = consumes_frame(prev, action)
    if f is None or len(goal_frames(prev)) <= 1 or not will_move(prev, action):
        return None
    mine = {(f[0] + 1 + dr, f[1] + 1 + dc) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}
    return {(r, c): FLOOR for (r, c) in _region(f) if (r, c) not in mine}

from _lib import me, entering_goal, hud_pattern, goal_pattern

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") is None:
        return "NA"
    if me(prev) is None:
        return "NA"
    eg = entering_goal(prev, action)
    gp = goal_pattern(prev)
    if eg is None or gp is None:
        return "NA"
    should = bool(eg and hud_pattern(prev) == gp)
    did = ctx["level_next"] != ctx["level_prev"]
    return "TRUE" if should == did else "FALSE"

from _lib import me, entering_goal, hud_match

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") is None:
        return "NA"
    if me(prev) is None:
        return "NA"
    eg = entering_goal(prev, action)
    hm = hud_match(prev)
    if eg is None or hm is None:
        return "NA"
    should = bool(eg and hm)
    did = ctx["level_next"] != ctx["level_prev"]
    return "TRUE" if should == did else "FALSE"

不换关的充要条件：只有当 me 走进某个框内 5×5 区（entering_goal）且走前 HUD 与那个框的图样匹配（hud_match_at：形状相同、颜色也相同）时才换关（ctx.level 加 1）；其他任何转移 level 不变。

from _lib import me, target_cells, goal_cells, hud_match_at

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") is None:
        return "NA"
    if me(prev) is None:
        return "NA"
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    gcs = goal_cells(prev)
    if tc is None or not gcs:
        return "NA"
    b = tc[0]
    should = b in gcs and hud_match_at(prev, (b[0] - 1, b[1] - 1))
    did = ctx["level_next"] != ctx["level_prev"]
    return "TRUE" if should == did else "FALSE"

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") is None:
        return "NA"
    if me(prev) is None:
        return "NA"

from _lib import me, target_cells, goal_cells, goal_frames, hud_match_at

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_next") is None:
        return "NA"
    if me(prev) is None:
        return "NA"
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    gcs = goal_cells(prev)
    if tc is None or not gcs:
        return "NA"
    b = tc[0]
    should = b in gcs and hud_match_at(prev, (b[0] - 1, b[1] - 1)) and len(goal_frames(prev)) == 1
    did = ctx["level_next"] != ctx["level_prev"]
    return "TRUE" if should == did else "FALSE"

"""ACTION2 要么让玩家块 me 下移恰好 CELL 格、列不变，且离开的 5×5 区变成 FLOOR、到达区变成玩家图样；要么 me 不动。"""

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, FLOOR

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != FLOOR:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = {k: FLOOR for k in _pattern(*a)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    u = under(prev, a)
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != u[(r, c)]:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):      # 挡住或算不出：不开口，交给 L2
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = dict(under(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under, levels_up

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    u = under(prev, a)
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != u[(r, c)]:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = dict(under(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

"""ACTION2 要么让玩家块 me 下移恰好 CELL 格、列不变，且到达区变成玩家图样、离开区恢复成被盖住的底（under：帧里看不见开关就按开关图样还原，其余格块外两侧皆色 5 则 5，否则 FLOOR）；要么 me 不动。"""

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under, levels_up, alive

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    u = under(prev, a)
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != u[(r, c)]:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = dict(under(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under, under_paint, under_known, levels_up, alive, landing

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    ok = False
    for u in (under(prev, a), under_paint(prev, a)):
        if u is not None and all(nxt[r][c] == u[(r, c)] for (r, c) in left):
            ok = True
    return "TRUE" if ok else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under_known, leave_ok, levels_up, alive, landing

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    return "TRUE" if leave_ok(prev, a, nxt, left) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under_known, leave_ok, levels_up, alive, landing, consumes_frame

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    return "TRUE" if leave_ok(prev, a, nxt, left) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a, (ctx or {}).get("level_prev")))
    f = consumes_frame(prev, action)
    if f is not None:                                            # 进匹配的框那一步整个框消失（frame_consumed 管那 7×7），离开区里属于框的格不认领
        out = {k: v for k, v in out.items() if not (f[0] <= k[0] <= f[0] + 6 and f[1] <= k[1] <= f[1] + 6)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

ACT = "ACTION2"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():

def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a, (ctx or {}).get("level_prev")))
    f = consumes_frame(prev, action)
    if f is not None:                                            # 进匹配的框那一步整个框消失（frame_consumed 管那 7×7），离开区里属于框的格不认领
        out = {k: v for k, v in out.items() if not (f[0] <= k[0] <= f[0] + 6 and f[1] <= k[1] <= f[1] + 6)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

"""ACTION3 要么让玩家块 me 左移恰好 CELL 格、行不变，且离开的 5×5 区变成 FLOOR、到达区变成玩家图样；要么 me 不动。"""

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, FLOOR

ACT = "ACTION3"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != FLOOR:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = {k: FLOOR for k in _pattern(*a)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

"""ACTION3 要么让玩家块 me 左移恰好 CELL 格、行不变，且离开的 5×5 区变成 FLOOR、到达区变成玩家图样；要么 me 不动。"""

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, FLOOR, will_move

ACT = "ACTION3"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != FLOOR:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):      # 挡住或算不出：不开口，交给 L2
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = {k: FLOOR for k in _pattern(*a)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under

ACT = "ACTION3"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    u = under(prev, a)
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != u[(r, c)]:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):      # 挡住或算不出：不开口，交给 L2
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = dict(under(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under, under_paint, under_known, levels_up, alive, landing

ACT = "ACTION3"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    ok = False
    for u in (under(prev, a), under_paint(prev, a)):
        if u is not None and all(nxt[r][c] == u[(r, c)] for (r, c) in left):
            ok = True
    return "TRUE" if ok else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under_known, leave_ok, levels_up, alive, landing

ACT = "ACTION3"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    return "TRUE" if leave_ok(prev, a, nxt, left) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under_known, leave_ok, levels_up, alive, landing, consumes_frame

ACT = "ACTION3"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    return "TRUE" if leave_ok(prev, a, nxt, left) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a, (ctx or {}).get("level_prev")))
    f = consumes_frame(prev, action)
    if f is not None:                                            # 进匹配的框那一步整个框消失（frame_consumed 管那 7×7），离开区里属于框的格不认领
        out = {k: v for k, v in out.items() if not (f[0] <= k[0] <= f[0] + 6 and f[1] <= k[1] <= f[1] + 6)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None

    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a, (ctx or {}).get("level_prev")))

    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, FLOOR

ACT = "ACTION4"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != FLOOR:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = {k: FLOOR for k in _pattern(*a)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under

ACT = "ACTION4"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    u = under(prev, a)
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != u[(r, c)]:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):      # 挡住或算不出：不开口，交给 L2
        return None

    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = dict(under(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    u = under(prev, a)
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != u[(r, c)]:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):      # 挡住或算不出：不开口，交给 L2
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = dict(under(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under, under_paint, under_known, levels_up, alive, landing

ACT = "ACTION4"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    ok = False
    for u in (under(prev, a), under_paint(prev, a)):
        if u is not None and all(nxt[r][c] == u[(r, c)] for (r, c) in left):
            ok = True
    return "TRUE" if ok else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

        return "NA"

        return "NA"

        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"

    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"

        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    return "TRUE" if leave_ok(prev, a, nxt, left) else "FALSE"

        return None

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under_known, leave_ok, levels_up, alive, landing, consumes_frame

ACT = "ACTION4"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    return "TRUE" if leave_ok(prev, a, nxt, left) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a, (ctx or {}).get("level_prev")))
    f = consumes_frame(prev, action)
    if f is not None:                                            # 进匹配的框那一步整个框消失（frame_consumed 管那 7×7），离开区里属于框的格不认领
        out = {k: v for k, v in out.items() if not (f[0] <= k[0] <= f[0] + 6 and f[1] <= k[1] <= f[1] + 6)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, FLOOR

ACT = "ACTION1"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != FLOOR:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = {k: FLOOR for k in _pattern(*a)}
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):      # 挡住或算不出：不开口，交给 L2
        return None

"""ACTION1 要么让玩家块 me 上移恰好 CELL 格、列不变，且到达区变成玩家图样、离开区恢复成被盖住的底（under 从块外同行/同列两侧推断：两侧皆色 5 则 5，否则 FLOOR）；要么 me 不动。"""

SCOPE = "只看 ACTION1；换关返回 NA；me(prev) 或 me(nxt) 定位不到返回 NA。只断言 me 的位移与 me 覆盖的两块 5×5，不管其他格。"

from _lib import me, CELL, DELTA, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under

ACT = "ACTION1"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) != d:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    u = under(prev, a)
    for (r, c) in _pattern(*a):
        if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b) and nxt[r][c] != u[(r, c)]:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):      # 挡住或算不出：不开口，交给 L2
        return None
    d = DELTA[ACT]
    b = (a[0] + d[0], a[1] + d[1])
    if not (0 <= b[0] <= len(prev) - CELL and 0 <= b[1] <= len(prev[0]) - CELL):
        return None
    out = dict(under(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL, ME_TOP, ME_BOT, will_move, under, under_paint, under_known, levels_up, alive, landing

ACT = "ACTION1"


def _pattern(t, l):
    return {(t + dr, l + dc): (ME_TOP if dr < 2 else ME_BOT) for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action != ACT:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    ok = False
    for u in (under(prev, a), under_paint(prev, a)):
        if u is not None and all(nxt[r][c] == u[(r, c)] for (r, c) in left):
            ok = True
    return "TRUE" if ok else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if a == b:
        return "TRUE"
    tgt = landing(prev, action)
    if tgt is None:
        return "NA"
    if b != tgt:
        return "FALSE"
    for (r, c), v in _pattern(*b).items():
        if nxt[r][c] != v:
            return "FALSE"
    left = [(r, c) for (r, c) in _pattern(*a) if (r, c) not in _pattern(*b)]
    return "TRUE" if leave_ok(prev, a, nxt, left) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action != ACT:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action) or levels_up(prev, action):   # 挡住、算不出、或换关整屏重画：不开口
        return None
    b = landing(prev, action)
    if b is None:
        return None
    out = dict(under_known(prev, a))
    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

        return None

    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None

    out.update(_pattern(*b))
    return out

        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None or not alive(prev):
        return None

mover_shiftsL6 · 会自己变的东西在册4 版
巡逻的开关沿固定的线来回走
每条转移里，prev 的每个开关在 nxt 里要么原位、要么沿行移了恰好 CELL 格、要么被 me 踩住（pressed_toggle）；nxt 里新出现的开关要么是 me 离开露出来的、要么是从相邻 CELL 格（含 me 底下）移来的；本关登记过掉头两端（MOVERS）的行，开关不越过两端。

from _lib import me, toggles, pressed_toggle, alive, CELL, MOVER_BOUNDS

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    tp, tn = set(toggles(prev)), set(toggles(nxt))
    pressed = pressed_toggle(prev, nxt)
    gone = tp - tn - ({pressed} if pressed else set())      # prev 里有、nxt 里不在原位、也没被踩：必须是移走了
    new = tn - tp
    revealed = {t for t in new if t == a}
    moved_in = new - revealed
    for t in list(gone) + list(moved_in):
        if not (MOVER_BOUNDS[0] <= t[1] <= MOVER_BOUNDS[1]):
            return "FALSE"
    for t in gone:
        if not any(u[0] == t[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for u in moved_in):
            return "FALSE"
    for u in moved_in:
        if not any(t[0] == u[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for t in tp):
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):

        return "NA"

from _lib import me, toggles, pressed_toggle, alive, CELL, MOVERS

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    lvl = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tp, tn = set(toggles(prev)), set(toggles(nxt))
    pressed = pressed_toggle(prev, nxt, lvl)
    gone = tp - tn - ({pressed} if pressed else set())
    new = tn - tp
    revealed = {t for t in new if t == a}
    moved_in = new - revealed
    tracks = MOVERS.get(lvl, {})
    for t in list(gone) + list(moved_in):
        bounds = tracks.get(t[0])
        if bounds is not None and not (bounds[0] <= t[1] <= bounds[1]):
            return "FALSE"
    for t in gone:
        if not any(u[0] == t[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for u in list(moved_in) + [b]):
            return "FALSE"
    for u in moved_in:
        if not any(t[0] == u[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for t in list(tp) + [a]):
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"

FOG_LEVELS = {6}

from _lib import me, toggles, pressed_toggle, alive, CELL, MOVERS

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if ctx.get("level_prev") in FOG_LEVELS:
        return "NA"                                   # 迷雾关里开关随视野出没，位置连续性无从谈起
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    lvl = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tp, tn = set(toggles(prev)), set(toggles(nxt))
    pressed = pressed_toggle(prev, nxt, lvl)
    gone = tp - tn - ({pressed} if pressed else set())
    new = tn - tp
    revealed = {t for t in new if t == a}
    moved_in = new - revealed
    tracks = MOVERS.get(lvl, {})
    for t in list(gone) + list(moved_in):
        bounds = tracks.get(t[0])
        if bounds is not None and not (bounds[0] <= t[1] <= bounds[1]):
            return "FALSE"
    for t in gone:
        if not any(u[0] == t[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for u in list(moved_in) + [b]):
            return "FALSE"
    for u in moved_in:
        if not any(t[0] == u[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for t in list(tp) + [a]):
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"

"""每条转移里，prev 的每个开关在 nxt 里要么原位、要么沿行移了恰好 CELL 格、要么被 me 踩住（pressed_toggle）；nxt 里新出现的开关要么是 me 离开露出来的、要么是从相邻 CELL 格（含 me 底下）移来的；本关登记过掉头两端（MOVERS）的行，开关不越过两端。"""

SCOPE = "只看 ACTION1–4；换关返回 NA；energy(prev)==0 返回 NA；me(prev)/me(nxt) 定位不到返回 NA；要 ctx.level_prev，没有返回 NA。只断言开关的位置变化，不断言 HUD。"
NOTE = "第 4 关（第 263 步起）有一个每步沿行来回移 5 格的十字开关，14↔24 列之间掉头；另一个开关不动。v3：第 5 关两个开关都动（第 343–346 步），v2 的「只有第 4 关那一行、那两端」被推翻——端点改成按关按行查 MOVERS 表，没登记的行只断言步长不断言端点。"

FOG_LEVELS = {6}

from _lib import me, toggles, pressed_toggle, alive, CELL, MOVERS

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if ctx.get("level_prev") in FOG_LEVELS:
        return "NA"                                   # 迷雾关里开关随视野出没，位置连续性无从谈起
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    lvl = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    tp, tn = set(toggles(prev)), set(toggles(nxt))
    pressed = pressed_toggle(prev, nxt, lvl)
    gone = tp - tn - ({pressed} if pressed else set())
    new = tn - tp
    revealed = {t for t in new if t == a}
    moved_in = new - revealed
    tracks = MOVERS.get(lvl, {})
    for t in list(gone) + list(moved_in):
        bounds = tracks.get(t[0])
        if bounds is not None and not (bounds[0] <= t[1] <= bounds[1]):
            return "FALSE"
    for t in gone:
        if not any(u[0] == t[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for u in list(moved_in) + [b]):
            return "FALSE"
    for u in moved_in:
        if not any(t[0] == u[0] and abs(u[1] - t[1]) == CELL for t in list(tp) + [a]):
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"
paint_orbitsL6 · 会自己变的东西在册2 版
有一关的染色物件绕固定的圈走
每条转移里，prev 里可见的染色物件在 nxt 里要么在 paint_next 算出的位置（本关登记了轨道 PAINT_ORBIT 就沿轨道走一格，没登记就原地）、要么被 me 盖住（me(nxt) == 那个位置）；nxt 里新出现的染色物件必是 me 离开露出来的。
v1 · 2026-09-15T20:40:40 · L6
每条转移里，prev 里可见的染色物件在 nxt 里要么在 paint_next 算出的位置（本关登记了轨道 PAINT_ORBIT 就沿轨道走一格，没登记就原地）、要么被 me 盖住（me(nxt) == 那个位置）；nxt 里新出现的染色物件必是 me 离开露出来的。
第 0–4 关它不动。第 5 关（第 343 步起）它绕一个 3×3 格的方块逆时针转、每步一格、周期 8——轨道是从 47 步读数抄下来的循环表，不是从帧算的；别的关有别的轨道就让它挨打。

"""每条转移里，prev 里可见的染色物件在 nxt 里要么在 paint_next 算出的位置（本关登记了轨道 PAINT_ORBIT 就沿轨道走一格，没登记就原地）、要么被 me 盖住（me(nxt) == 那个位置）；nxt 里新出现的染色物件必是 me 离开露出来的。"""

from _lib import me, paints, paint_next, alive

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if not ctx or ctx.get("level_prev") is None or ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    pp, pn = paints(prev), paints(nxt)
    if not pp:
        return "NA"
    lvl = int(ctx["level_prev"])
    for p in pp:
        q = paint_next(p, lvl)
        if q is None:
            return "FALSE"
        if q != b and q not in pn:
            return "FALSE"
    for q in pn:
        if q not in [paint_next(p, lvl) for p in pp] and q != a:
            return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE"

paint_recolors_hudL6 · 会自己变的东西在册7 版
踩染色物件，钥匙换下一种颜色
ACTION1–4：me 踩到染色物件（pressed_paint：prev 里可见、这一步被 me 盖住——它会动的关里是它走到 me 落点）时，HUD 图样形状不变、HUD 颜色按 PAINT_NEXT 表换一档（12→9→14→8→12 四色循环）；其余转移 hud_ink 不变。染色物件是持久物件（离开后原样露出）。
v1 · 2026-09-15T17:51:36 · L6
ACTION1–4：me 移进一个染色物件（paints）的目标格时，HUD 图样形状不变、HUD 颜色（hud_ink）变成 PAINT_INK；踩过的染色物件消失（该格变成玩家图样，离开后按 under 还原为 FLOOR）；其余转移 hud_ink 不变。
第 145 步：HUD 图样区 20 格 12→9，形状原样；物件的色 14/8/0 格全变成玩家图样。只观测过一次、一种颜色，PAINT_INK=9 是从这一次读出来的；别的关有别的颜色就让它挨打。
v1 源码
"""ACTION1–4：me 移进一个染色物件（paints）的目标格时，HUD 图样形状不变、HUD 颜色（hud_ink）变成 PAINT_INK；踩过的染色物件消失（该格变成玩家图样，离开后按 under 还原为 FLOOR）；其余转移 hud_ink 不变。"""
LEVEL = "L6"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION1–4；换关返回 NA；energy(prev)==0 返回 NA；me(prev)/me(nxt) 定位不到或目标区算不出返回 NA；hud_ink 读不出（不止一色）返回 NA。"
NOTE = "第 145 步：HUD 图样区 20 格 12→9，形状原样；物件的色 14/8/0 格全变成玩家图样。只观测过一次、一种颜色，PAINT_INK=9 是从这一次读出来的；别的关有别的颜色就让它挨打。"
EVIDENCE = ["5d2f255f6bb3164b"]

from _lib import me, hud_pattern, hud_ink, steps_on_paint, alive, will_move, PAINT_INK, HUD_BG

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    pt = steps_on_paint(prev, action)
    i0, i1 = hud_ink(prev), hud_ink(nxt)
    if pt is None or i0 is None or i1 is None:
        return "NA"
    if pt and a != b:
        if hud_pattern(prev) != hud_pattern(nxt):
            return "FALSE"
        return "TRUE" if i1 == PAINT_INK else "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if i1 == i0 else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    """踩染色物件那一步认领 HUD 图样区（rows 55–60, cols 3–8）的 36 格：形状照旧、非底色格换成 PAINT_INK。"""
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if me(prev) is None or not steps_on_paint(prev, action) or not will_move(prev, action):
        return None
    p = hud_pattern(prev)
    out = {}
    for i in range(3):
        for j in range(3):
            v = PAINT_INK if p[3 * i + j] else HUD_BG
            for dr in range(2):
                for dc in range(2):
                    out[(55 + 2 * i + dr, 3 + 2 * j + dc)] = v
    return out

第 145 步：HUD 图样区 20 格 12→9，形状原样；物件的色 14/8/0 格被玩家盖住。只观测过一次、一种颜色，PAINT_INK=9 是从这一次读出来的；别的关有别的颜色就让它挨打。v1 写「踩过就消失」，第 146 步离开它原样露出来——主张里删掉那句（源码没改，主张是错的部分不该留）。

from _lib import me, hud_pattern, hud_ink, steps_on_paint, alive, will_move, PAINT_INK, HUD_BG

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    pt = steps_on_paint(prev, action)
    i0, i1 = hud_ink(prev), hud_ink(nxt)
    if pt is None or i0 is None or i1 is None:
        return "NA"
    if pt and a != b:
        if hud_pattern(prev) != hud_pattern(nxt):
            return "FALSE"
        return "TRUE" if i1 == PAINT_INK else "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if i1 == i0 else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    """踩染色物件那一步认领 HUD 图样区（rows 55–60, cols 3–8）的 36 格：形状照旧、非底色格换成 PAINT_INK。"""
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if me(prev) is None or not steps_on_paint(prev, action) or not will_move(prev, action):
        return None
    p = hud_pattern(prev)
    out = {}
    for i in range(3):
        for j in range(3):
            v = PAINT_INK if p[3 * i + j] else HUD_BG
            for dr in range(2):
                for dc in range(2):
                    out[(55 + 2 * i + dr, 3 + 2 * j + dc)] = v

from _lib import me, hud_pattern, hud_ink, steps_on_paint, alive, will_move, PAINT_NEXT, HUD_BG

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    pt = steps_on_paint(prev, action)
    i0, i1 = hud_ink(prev), hud_ink(nxt)
    if pt is None or i0 is None or i1 is None:
        return "NA"
    if pt and a != b:
        if i0 not in PAINT_NEXT:
            return "NA"
        if hud_pattern(prev) != hud_pattern(nxt):
            return "FALSE"
        return "TRUE" if i1 == PAINT_NEXT[i0] else "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if i1 == i0 else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    """踩染色物件那一步认领 HUD 图样区（rows 55–60, cols 3–8）的 36 格：形状照旧、非底色格换成 PAINT_NEXT[旧色]。"""
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    if me(prev) is None or not steps_on_paint(prev, action) or not will_move(prev, action):
        return None
    i0 = hud_ink(prev)
    if i0 not in PAINT_NEXT:
        return None
    p = hud_pattern(prev)
    out = {}
    for i in range(3):
        for j in range(3):
            v = PAINT_NEXT[i0] if p[3 * i + j] else HUD_BG
            for dr in range(2):
                for dc in range(2):
                    out[(55 + 2 * i + dr, 3 + 2 * j + dc)] = v
    return out

from _lib import me, hud_pattern, hud_ink, pressed_paint, steps_on_paint, alive, will_move, PAINT_NEXT, PAINT_ORBIT, HUD_BG

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    i0, i1 = hud_ink(prev), hud_ink(nxt)
    if i0 is None or i1 is None:
        return "NA"
    if pressed_paint(prev, nxt, ctx.get("level_prev") if ctx else None) is not None and not (ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") in FOG_LEVELS and steps_on_paint(prev, action) is False):
        if i0 not in PAINT_NEXT:
            return "NA"
        if hud_pattern(prev) != hud_pattern(nxt):
            return "FALSE"
        return "TRUE" if i1 == PAINT_NEXT[i0] else "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if i1 == i0 else "FALSE"


FOG_LEVELS = {6}   # 迷雾关：染色物件出视野也会让 pressed_paint 误判（第 977 步），只认「目标格里就是它」那种踩法


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    """本关染色物件不动时，踩它那一步认领 HUD 图样区（rows 55–60, cols 3–8）的 36 格：形状照旧、非底色格换成 PAINT_NEXT[旧色]。"""
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    lvl = (ctx or {}).get("level_prev")
    if lvl is None or lvl in PAINT_ORBIT:
        return None
    if me(prev) is None or not steps_on_paint(prev, action) or not will_move(prev, action):
        return None
    i0 = hud_ink(prev)
    if i0 not in PAINT_NEXT:
        return None
    p = hud_pattern(prev)
    out = {}
    for i in range(3):
        for j in range(3):
            v = PAINT_NEXT[i0] if p[3 * i + j] else HUD_BG
            for dr in range(2):
                for dc in range(2):
                    out[(55 + 2 * i + dr, 3 + 2 * j + dc)] = v
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):

        return "NA"

    if not mv and any(prev[r][c] != nxt[r][c] for r in range(60) for c in range(64)):
        return "FALSE"                              # 不动时主区（行 < 60）不变；能量条那两行由 energy_cost/energy_tick 管
    return "TRUE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    """不动时认领 me 自己的 25 格（颜色不变）；动的情况交给 L1。"""
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return None
    mv = will_move(prev, action)
    if mv is None or mv:
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    return {(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc): prev[a[0] + dr][a[1] + dc] for dr in range(5) for dc in range(5)}

from _lib import energy, me, target_cells, will_move, alive, levels_up, REFILL, FULL_ENERGY, HUD_ROW, HUD_COLOR

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def _on_refill(prev, action):
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None:
        return None
    return any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    rf = _on_refill(prev, action)
    if not rf or a == b:
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if energy(nxt) == FULL_ENERGY else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev) or levels_up(prev, action):
        return None
    rf = _on_refill(prev, action)
    if not rf or not will_move(prev, action):
        return None
    # 能量条满格：第 61、62 行 cols 14..55（首帧读出 42 格的位置）
    cols = [c for c, v in enumerate(prev[HUD_ROW]) if v == HUD_COLOR]
    right = max(cols) if cols else 55
    out = {}
    for c in range(right - FULL_ENERGY + 1, right + 1):
        out[(HUD_ROW, c)] = HUD_COLOR
        out[(HUD_ROW + 1, c)] = HUD_COLOR
    return out

from _lib import me, target_cells, energy, alive, REFILL
from geo import cells_of, components

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def _refills(g):
    """主区里 3×3 的色 REFILL 物件（9 格连通块）的个数；能量条是长条，不算。"""
    return sum(1 for comp in components(cells_of(g, REFILL)) if len(comp) == 9)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None:
        return "NA"
    hit = any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc) and a != b
    if not alive(prev) and not hit:
        return "NA"
    n0, n1 = _refills(prev), _refills(nxt)
    if hit:
        return "TRUE" if n1 == n0 - 1 else "FALSE"      # 被 me 盖住那一步个数就少一（盖住的不成块）
    # 没踩补给：me 离开的格若原来盖着补给，也不会露出来——所以个数只能不变
    return "TRUE" if n1 == n0 else "FALSE"

    tc = target_cells(prev, action)
    if tc is None:
        return "NA"
    hit = any(prev[r][c] == REFILL for r, c in tc) and a != b
    if not alive(prev) and not hit:
        return "NA"
    n0, n1 = _refills(prev), _refills(nxt)
    if hit:
        return "TRUE" if n1 == n0 - 1 else "FALSE"      # 被 me 盖住那一步个数就少一（盖住的不成块）
    # 没踩补给：me 离开的格若原来盖着补给，也不会露出来——所以个数只能不变
    return "TRUE" if n1 == n0 else "FALSE"

from geo import cells_of, components

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def _refills(g):
    cs = [(r, c) for (r, c) in cells_of(g, REFILL) if r < 60]
    return sum(1 for comp in components(cs) if len(comp) == 8)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)

SCOPE = "只看 ACTION1–4；换关返回 NA；energy(prev)==0 返回 NA（重置步由 death_reset 管）；me(prev)/me(nxt) 定位不到或目标区算不出返回 NA；目标格是染色物件（steps_on_paint）返回 NA。只断言「变/不变」，怎么变另写。"
NOTE = "v2：第 46 步能量 0 再动是掉命重置，HUD 整体重画，缩出 scope。v3：第 145 步走进染色物件，HUD 图样区 20 格换色（12→9）但形状没变；开关改成按形状认，染色步缩出 scope。v4：第 278 步会动的开关与 me 同时到同一格、HUD 转了——「踩到」从「prev 目标格有开关」改成「这一步有一个开关被 me 盖住」（pressed_toggle）。"
EVIDENCE = ["2542e684fdabf403", "316352592eb5850c", "eedeb19e0d864112", "5d2f255f6bb3164b", "ac13f608150732b9", "aefb02c7d11426fd", "b209d9efc223d456", "c79200ae0f16e188"]

from _lib import me, hud_pattern, pressed_toggle, steps_on_paint, alive

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS:
        return "NA"
    if not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    pt = steps_on_paint(prev, action)
    if pt is None:
        return "NA"
    if pt and a != b:
        return "NA"
    changed = hud_pattern(prev) != hud_pattern(nxt)
    lvl = ctx.get("level_prev") if ctx else None
    hit = pressed_toggle(prev, nxt, lvl) is not None
    return "TRUE" if changed == hit else "FALSE"

非十字开关：按见过的对照表换钥匙
me 踩到一个非十字形开关（pressed_toggle 且非 toggle_is_cross；会动的开关走到 me 落点也算）时，新 HUD 图样 = TOGGLE_MAP[(开关内图样, 旧 HUD 图样)]，颜色不变；表里只登记观测过的组合，没见过的组合返回 NA；其余转移不受本条约束。
v1 · 2026-09-15T19:08:13 · L6
me 移进一个非十字形开关的目标格时，新 HUD 图样 = TOGGLE_MAP[(开关内图样, 旧 HUD 图样)]，颜色不变；表里只登记观测过的组合，没见过的组合返回 NA；其余转移不受本条约束。
接替 toggle_sets_hud（第 3 关一次观测像「设成目标」，第 4 关同形开关同输入同输出，但目标不同——推翻）。现在只登记「这形状、这输入 → 这输出」，是不是常量、是不是某种变换，让下一个不同输入来答。

"""me 移进一个非十字形开关的目标格时，新 HUD 图样 = TOGGLE_MAP[(开关内图样, 旧 HUD 图样)]，颜色不变；表里只登记观测过的组合，没见过的组合返回 NA；其余转移不受本条约束。"""

from _lib import me, hud_pattern, hud_ink, steps_on_toggle, steps_on_cross, target_cells, toggle_inner, will_move, alive, HUD_BG

ACTS = {"ACTION1", "ACTION2", "ACTION3", "ACTION4"}

# 键：(内 3×3 图样按行拼成的元组, 旧 HUD 9 元组) → 新 HUD 9 元组。第 219 步（第 3 关）与第 262 步（第 4 关）同一组合。
TOGGLE_MAP = {
    ((0, 3, 3, 3, 0, 0, 3, 0, 3), (False, True, False, True, True, False, False, True, True)):
        (True, True, True, False, False, True, True, False, True),
}


def _inner_key(prev, cell):
    inner = toggle_inner(prev, cell)
    return tuple(inner[(dr, dc)] for dr in range(1, 4) for dc in range(1, 4))


def _lookup(prev, action):
    """返回新图样；不是踩非十字开关返回 None；组合没见过返回 'unseen'。"""
    tg = steps_on_toggle(prev, action)
    cr = steps_on_cross(prev, action)
    if tg is None or cr is None or not tg or cr:
        return None
    cell = target_cells(prev, action)[0]
    key = (_inner_key(prev, cell), hud_pattern(prev))
    return TOGGLE_MAP.get(key, "unseen")


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx.get("level_prev") != ctx.get("level_next"):
        return "NA"
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None or a == b:
        return "NA"
    new = _lookup(prev, action)
    if new is None or new == "unseen":
        return "NA"
    return "TRUE" if hud_pattern(nxt) == new else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if action not in ACTS or not alive(prev) or me(prev) is None:
        return None
    if not will_move(prev, action):
        return None
    new = _lookup(prev, action)
    ink = hud_ink(prev)
    if new is None or new == "unseen" or ink is None:
        return None
    out = {}
    for i in range(3):
        for j in range(3):
            v = ink if new[3 * i + j] else HUD_BG
            for dr in range(2):
                for dc in range(2):
                    out[(55 + 2 * i + dr, 3 + 2 * j + dc)] = v
    return out

接替 toggle_sets_hud（第 3 关一次观测像「设成目标」，第 4 关同形开关同输入同输出，但目标不同——推翻）。现在只登记「这形状、这输入 → 这输出」，是不是常量、是不是某种变换，让下一个不同输入来答。
